<div class="wr-explained" data-widget="world-rankings-explained" data-script="world-rugby_world-rankings">
 <div class="wr-explained__wrapper">
 <div class="wr-explained__header">
 Rankings explained
 <button class="wr-explained__close-button js-close-button">
 <span class="u-screen-reader">Close</span>
 </button>
 </div>
 <div class="wr-explained__body">
 <h3 class="wr-explained__heading">Calculation</h3>
 <p class="wr-explained__copy">Men’s and Women's World Rugby Rankings are calculated using a 'Points Exchange' system, where sides gain or lose points based on the match result. Other criteria include the relative strength of each team, the margin of victory, and an allowance for home advantage.</p>
 <h3 class="wr-explained__heading">Member Unions</h3>
 <p class="wr-explained__copy">All member countries have a rating, typically between 0 and 100. The top side in the world will normally have a rating above 90. Any match that is not a full international between two member countries does not count at all.</p>
 <h3 class="wr-explained__heading">RWC Ranking</h3>
 <p class="wr-explained__copy">Points are doubled during the World Cup Finals to recognise the importance of this event, but all other international matches, friendly or competitive, are treated the same across the world.</p>
 <div class="wr-explained__button-container">
 <a href="/rankings/explanation" class="wr-explained__button">
 Full Explanation
 </a>
 </div>
 </div>
 </div>

 <a class="promo-full-width__wrapper-link" href="https://ql.e-c.al/4eRWjqY" target="_blank">
 <div class="promo-full-width ">
 <div class="promo-full-width__container">
 <div class="promo-full-width__content">
 <div class="promo-full-width__body">
 <h2 class="promo-full-width__title">
 Download all rugby fixtures to your calendar
 </h2>
 <p class="promo-full-width__summary">
 Get every fixture delivered to your calendar by subscribing to World Rugby's calendar to sync all matches to your device

 <h3 class="card__subtitle article-list__subtitle">
 World Rankings
 </h3>
 </div>
 <h2 class="card__title article-list__title">
 New World Rugby Rankings criteria
 </h2>
 <div class="card__summary article-list__summary">
 With effect from 1 July, 2026 the ‘home weighting’ aspect of the World Rugby Rankings will be removed to reflect an evolving competition landscape in the international game.
 </div>

 Canada will replace New Zealand in second place if they beat the Black Ferns – who have never been outside the top two – for the first time in history this weekend.
 </div>

 Lia Pineiro, Clara Piquero and Claudia Peña Hidalgo all crossed the whitewash as Spain beat the Netherlands 22-5 in the penultimate match of the Rugby Europe Women’s Championship 2024, while Andorra made an impressive entrance to test rugby.
 </div>

 <h3 class="card__subtitle article-list__subtitle">
 World Rankings

 <h2 class="card__title article-list__title">
 New World Rugby Rankings criteria
 </h2>
 <div class="card__summary article-list__summary">
 With effect from 1 July, 2026 the ‘home weighting’ aspect of the World Rugby Rankings will be removed to reflect an evolving competition landscape in the international game.
